00088: def cabinet(slot, position, size, bays=2, levels=2, angle=0):
00089:     """Open cabinet facing -Y; boards and back remain under one owner."""
00090:     w, d, h = size
00091:     x, y, z = position
00092:     a = Asset(slot)
00093:     t = .055
00094:     for key, zz in (("bottom", z+.10), ("top", z+h-t/2)):
00095:         a.include(key, box((w, d, t), (x,y,zz), LIGHT_WOOD))
00096:     for j, xx in enumerate((x-w/2+t/2, x+w/2-t/2)):
00097:         a.include(f"end_{j}", box((t,d,h-.10), (xx,y,z+(h+.10)/2), WOOD))
00098:     a.include("back", box((w-.10,.035,h-.15),
00099:                          (x,y+d/2-.025,z+(h+.10)/2), BACKING))
00100:     for j in range(1, levels):
00101:         zz = z+.10+(h-.13)*j/levels
00102:         a.include(f"shelf_{j}", box((w-.08,d-.015,t), (x,y,zz), LIGHT_WOOD))
00103:     for j in range(1, bays):
00104:         xx = x-w/2+w*j/bays
00105:         a.include(f"divider_{j}", box((t,d-.04,h-.15),
00106:                                     (xx,y,z+(h+.10)/2), WOOD))
00107:     for j, xx in enumerate((x-w*.38,x+w*.38)):
00108:         a.include(f"toe_{j}", box((.11,d*.78,.10), (xx,y,z+.05), WOOD))
00109:     if angle:
00110:         a.turn('z',angle,pivot=(x,y,z))
00111:     return scene.include(slot,a)

00225: # stools, round rug, trays/boxes/blocks, two plants and wall display.

00240: # Add restrained matte wood grain only within known semantic wood owners.
00241: wood_slots = [
00242:     "foreground_island","left_mid_shelf","left_front_shelf",
00243:     "back_left_shelf","back_middle_shelf","right_back_shelf",
00244:     "right_side_shelf","center_activity_table","right_activity_table"
00245: ]
00246: wood_palette=(WOOD,LIGHT_WOOD,EDGE,BACKING)
00247: processed=set()
00248: for slot in wood_slots:
00249:     marker=bpy.data.objects.get(slot)
00250:     assert marker is not None, "Missing semantic marker: "+slot
00251:     meshes=[o for o in marker.children_recursive if o.type=='MESH']
00252:     assert meshes, "Empty semantic owner: "+slot
00253:     for obj in meshes:
00254:         for ms in obj.material_slots:
00255:             mat=ms.material
00256:             if mat is None or mat.as_pointer() in processed:
00257:                 continue
00258:             processed.add(mat.as_pointer())
00259:             mat.use_nodes=True
00260:             bsdf=mat.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00261:             if bsdf:
00262:                 bsdf.inputs["Roughness"].default_value=.78
00263:                 nodes=mat.node_tree.nodes
00264:                 links=mat.node_tree.links

00219: # Pre-execution self-review:
00220: # Seven open cabinets, five windows, two tables, five stools, rug, materials,
00221: # two plants and wall display retained. Owners, support heights and occlusion fixed.
00222: # Approximately 350 primitives; tray rims/contents dominate repeated decoration.
00223: # Single rods preserve rug/stool curves; no fillets or densely sampled straight rods.
00224: # All dimensions finite/positive, endpoints distinct, include slots unique per parent.
00225: # No new geometry after realize. Camera correction alone changes projected overlap.
00226: # The successful render was pale overall, but appearance is deliberately held fixed
00227: # to assess framing first. Neither true color nor exact support follows from brightness.
00228: # Material setup below reproduces step 1's shared palette shader setup unchanged.
00229: # Markers resolve semantic owners and descendant meshes are asserted nonempty.
00230: # This is an inline code review, not a claim of collision or regression-test success.
00231: 
00232: realize(scene)
00233: 
00234: wood_slots = [
00235:     "foreground_island","left_mid_shelf","left_front_shelf",
00236:     "back_left_shelf","back_middle_shelf","right_back_shelf",
00237:     "right_side_shelf","center_activity_table","right_activity_table"
00238: ]
00239: processed = set()
00240: for slot in wood_slots:
00241:     marker = bpy.data.objects.get(slot)
00242:     assert marker is not None, "Missing semantic marker: "+slot
00243:     meshes = [o for o in marker.children_recursive if o.type == 'MESH']
00244:     assert meshes, "Empty semantic owner: "+slot
00245:     for obj in meshes:
00246:         for ms in obj.material_slots:
00247:             mat = ms.material
00248:             if mat is None or mat.as_pointer() in processed:
00249:                 continue
00250:             processed.add(mat.as_pointer())
00251:             mat.use_nodes = True
00252:             bsdf = mat.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00253:             if bsdf:
00254:                 bsdf.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = .78
00255:                 nodes,links = mat.node_tree.nodes,mat.node_tree.links

00173: def ribbon_mesh(name, paths, mat, y):
00174:     vertices, faces = [], []
00175:     for points, width in paths:
00176:         start = len(vertices)
00177:         for j, (x, z) in enumerate(points):
00178:             before = points[max(0, j-1)]
00179:             after = points[min(len(points)-1, j+1)]
00180:             dx, dz = after[0]-before[0], after[1]-before[1]
00181:             length = max(.00001, math.hypot(dx, dz))
00182:             w = width * (1-.60*j/max(1, len(points)-1))
00183:             nx, nz = -dz/length*w/2, dx/length*w/2
00184:             vertices.extend([(x+nx, y, z+nz), (x-nx, y, z-nz)])
00185:             if j:
00186:                 k = start+2*j
00187:                 faces.append((k-2, k-1, k+1, k))
00188:     mesh = bpy.data.meshes.new(name)
00189:     mesh.from_pydata(vertices, [], faces)
00190:     mesh.update()
00191:     ob = bpy.data.objects.new(name, mesh)
00192:     bpy.context.collection.objects.link(ob)
00193:     ob.data.materials.append(mat)
